This coincides with the last line of the expression we derived for the factors in (
43
), thus it is OLS when, for a fixed
t
, we regress
x_{it}
onto
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\bm{\lambda}_{i}
or, equivalently, onto
\widetilde{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}
because of (
46
).

Theorem 2 in
Bai 2003
for the loadings says,

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})

\displaystyle=\bm{V}_{nT}^{-1}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\bm{F}}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1).

This is the only expression reported by
Bai 2003
and it is analogous to the expression we derived for the loadings in the first line of (
7.1
), up to a factor
\bm{V}_{nT}^{-1}
.

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1).

(48)

This coincides with the last line of the expression we derived for the factors in (
7.1
), thus it is OLS when, for a fixed
i
, we regress
x_{it}
onto
\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}
or, equivalently, onto
\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}
because of (
45
), which is normalized such that
\frac{\widetilde{\bm{F}}^{\prime}\widetilde{\bm{F}}}{T}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
.

Moreover, let
\bm{Q}_{1}=\bm{V}_{0}^{1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})^{-1/2}
, where
\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}
is the
r\times r
matrix having as columns the normalized eigenvectors of
(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})^{1/2}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})^{1/2}
, and
\bm{V}_{0}
is the
r\times r
matrix of corresponding eigenvalues sorted in descending order. Then, it is shown that

\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})\to_{d}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0}_{r},\bm{\Theta}_{i}^{B}\right),

(49)

where
\bm{\Theta}_{i}^{B}=(\bm{Q}_{1}^{-1})^{\prime}\bm{\Phi}_{i}\bm{Q}_{1}^{-1}
. And

\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widetilde{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}{\mathbf{F}}_{t})\to_{d}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0}_{r},\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{B}\right),

(50)

where
\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{B}=\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{Q}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}
.

Now note that, since
\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}
are normalized eigenvectors of
(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}
, then

\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{\prime}=\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{\prime}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
and

\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}=(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0},

(51)

and, since
\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}
are normalized eigenvectors of
\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}
, then
\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}=\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
and

\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}=\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}.

(52)

Let us compare the asymptotic covariances in Theorems
1
and
2
with the expressions by
Bai 2003
.
From Theorem
1
(a) and using the definition of
\bm{Q}_{0}

\displaystyle\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Theta}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\bm{Q}_{0}\bm{\Phi}_{i}\bm{Q}_{0}^{\prime}\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\bm{\Phi}_{i}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left((\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\bm{\Phi}_{i}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left((\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Phi}_{i}\right).

(53)

From (
49
) and using the definition of
\bm{Q}_{1}
and (
52
)

\displaystyle\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Theta}_{i}^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left((\bm{Q}_{1}^{-1})^{\prime}\bm{\Phi}_{i}\bm{Q}_{1}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{V}_{0}^{-1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{\Phi}_{i}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}\bm{V}_{0}^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{\Phi}_{i}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Phi}_{i}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Phi}_{i}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left((\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Phi}_{i}\right).

(54)

From Theorem
2
(a) and using the definition of
\bm{Q}_{0}
and (
51
)

\displaystyle\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Pi}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left((\bm{Q}_{0}^{-1})^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{Q}_{0}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left((\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left((\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\right).

(55)

From (
50
) and using the definition of
\bm{Q}_{1}
and (
52
)

\displaystyle\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\bm{Q}_{1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{Q}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{V}_{0}^{-1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}\bm{V}_{0}^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{V}_{0}^{-1}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=\text{tr}\left((\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\right).

(56)

Thus,

\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Theta}_{i}\right)=\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Theta}_{i}^{B}\right),\hskip 20.00003pt\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Pi}_{t}\right)=\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Pi}_{t}^{B}\right).

8
Asymptotics for the common and idiosyncratic components

For any given
i=1,\ldots,n
,
t=1,\ldots,T
, the estimator of the common component is

\widehat{\chi}_{it}=\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t},

(57)

Theorem 3

(Asymptotic Normality of common component)
.

Under Assumptions
1
through
5
(a) and
5
(b) as
n,T\to\infty
, for any given
i=1,\ldots,n
and
t=1,\ldots,T
,

(a)

\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})|\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it}|=O_{\mathrm{P}}(1)
;

(b)

(\frac{1}{n}V_{it}+\frac{1}{T}W_{it})^{-1/2}(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})\to_{d}\mathcal{N}\left(0,1\right)
,
where
V_{it}=\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\lambda}_{i}
and
W_{it}=\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Phi}_{i}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}
.

Proof of Theorem
3
.
From definition (
57
), Propositions
3
(a) and
6
(a), have

\displaystyle\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t})+(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t})+(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}+(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t})

\displaystyle=\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t})+(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{T}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t})+\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i})+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{T}\right)\right).

(58)

Part (a) follows from Propositions
3
(a) and
6
(a).

For part (b), by using (
31
) and the second line of (
7.1
)

\displaystyle\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i})=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)

\displaystyle+\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{T},\frac{1}{\sqrt{nT}}\right)\right)

(59)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

Similarly, by using (
40
) and the last line of (
43
)

\displaystyle\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t})=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\left(\frac{{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\xi_{it}\right)

\displaystyle+\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{nT}}\right)\right)

(60)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\left(\frac{{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\xi_{it}\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

By using (
59
) and (
60
) into (
58
),

\displaystyle\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\left(\frac{{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\xi_{it}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right).

(61)

Now, by Assumptions
1
(a) and
5
(b),
as
n\to\infty
,

\eta_{it,nT}=\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\left(\frac{{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\xi_{it}\right)\to_{d}\mathcal{N}(0,\underbrace{\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{\lambda}_{i}}_{V_{it}}).

(62)

Similarly, by Assumptions
1
(c) and
5
(a), as
T\to\infty
,

\theta_{it,nT}=\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)\to_{d}\mathcal{N}(0,\underbrace{\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Phi}_{i}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}}_{W_{it}}).

(63)

Moreover,
\eta_{it,nT}
and
\theta_{it,nT}
are asymptotically independent because the former is the sum of cross-section random variables at time
t
and the latter is the sum of the
i
th time series.

Let
a_{nT}=\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\sqrt{n}}
and
b_{nT}=\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\sqrt{T}}
. Then, from (
61
),

\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})=a_{nT}\eta_{it,nT}+b_{nT}\theta_{it,nT}+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right).

(64)

Following the same arguments in the proof of Theorem 3 in
Bai 2003
, because of (
62
) and (
63
), as
n,T\to\infty
, we have

a_{nT}\eta_{it,nT}+b_{nT}\theta_{it,nT}\to_{d}\mathcal{N}(0,a_{nT}^{2}V_{it}+b_{nT}^{2}W_{it})

(65)

and because of (
64
) and (
65
), as
n,T\to\infty
,

\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})\to_{d}\mathcal{N}(0,a_{nT}^{2}V_{it}+b_{nT}^{2}W_{it}),

which is equivalent to

\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})}{\sqrt{a_{nT}^{2}V_{it}+b_{nT}^{2}W_{it}}}\to_{d}\mathcal{N}(0,1).

(66)

Now,

\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\sqrt{a_{nT}^{2}V_{it}+b_{nT}^{2}W_{it}}}=\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})}{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}V_{it}+\frac{1}{T}W_{it}}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}V_{it}+\frac{1}{T}W_{it}}}.

So, from (
66
), as
n,T\to\infty
,

\frac{\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})}{\sqrt{a_{nT}^{2}V_{it}+b_{nT}^{2}W_{it}}}=\frac{(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}V_{it}+\frac{1}{T}W_{it}}}\to_{d}\mathcal{N}(0,1).

This completes the proof.
\Box

The estimator of the idiosyncratic component is:

\widehat{\xi}_{it}=x_{it}-\widehat{\chi}_{it},

Clearly, then

\widehat{\xi}_{it}-{\xi}_{it}=(x_{it}-\widehat{\chi}_{it})-(x_{it}-\chi_{it})=\chi_{it}-\widehat{\chi}_{it},

(67)

and we have
\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})
-consistency. Notice that the estimator of the idiosyncratic covariance matrix given by the sample covariance is

\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{\xi}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{t}\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{t}^{\prime}=\widehat{\mathbf{W}}^{x}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{x}\widehat{\mathbf{W}}^{x\prime},

(68)

where
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{x}
is the
(n-r)\times(n-r)
diagonal matrix with diagonal entries
\widehat{\mu}_{j}^{x}
,
j=r+1,\ldots,n
, and
\widehat{\mathbf{W}}^{x}
is the
n\times(n-r)
matrix having as columns the corresponding normalized eigenvectors. Therefore, such estimator has rank
\min(n-r,T)
and even if
n<T
it can never be a consistent estimators. An alternative based on shrinking is given in
Fan
et al. 2013
.

9
Asymptotics under identifying constraints

Let us make the classical identifying assumptions used for exploratory factor analysis.

Assumption 9
.

(a)

For all
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}
diagonal.

(b)

For all
T\in\mathbb{N}

\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
.

This assumption has two main implications.

Proposition 7
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
9

(a)

\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}=\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}
, for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
;

(b)

\bm{\Lambda}=\mathbf{V}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}
, for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
;

(c)

\mathbf{F}_{t}=(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\chi}_{t}
for all
t=1,\ldots,T
and all
n\in\mathbb{N}
;

(d)

\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}=\bm{V}_{0}
;

(e)

\bm{\Gamma}^{F}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
.

Proposition 8
.

Under Assumptions
1
through
4
, and Assumptions
5
(c) and
9
, as
n,T\to\infty

(a)

\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})\|\widehat{\mathbf{H}}-\bm{J}\|=o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)
;

(b)

\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})\|\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}-\bm{J}\|=o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)
;

where
\bm{J}
is a diagonal
r\times r
matrix with entries
\pm 1
.

Note that
Bai and Ng 2013
prove this with a rate
\min(n,\sqrt{nT},T)=\min(n,T)
based on Lemma B2 of
Bai 2003
. Here we prove it with a rate
\min(\sqrt{nT},T)
(we do not have the term
n
). The only thing that matters is that the rate is smaller than
\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})
.
We now show that, in this case, PCA is equivalent to OLS, i.e. as if, when estimating the loadings, the factors were known, and, when estimating the factors, the loadings were known.

For the loadings, using Proposition
8
(a) in the second line of (
7.1
), we get (note that
\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O(1)
by Assumption
1
(a))

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i})+\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}-\bm{J})\bm{\lambda}_{i}+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\bm{J}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\bm{J}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle\to_{d}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0}_{r},(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\bm{\Phi}_{i}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\right).

(69)

Notice that,
\bm{J}
plays no role in the covariance since
\bm{J}
is diagonal and
\bm{J}^{2}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
, and, in fact, here
\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
and
\bm{\Gamma}^{F}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
so both could be omitted in (
9
).

From (
9
) it is clear that the PC estimator is asymptotically equivalent to the OLS estimator,
{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}
, we get when, for fixed
i
, we regress
x_{it}
onto
\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}
. Indeed,

\displaystyle{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{J}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}x_{it}\right)-\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{J}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{J}\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}+\xi_{it})\right)-\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{J}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{J}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right).

(70)

For the factors using Proposition
8
(b) in the last line of (
43
), we get (note that
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}(1)
by Lemma
2
(ii))

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t})+\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}-\bm{J})\mathbf{F}_{t}+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\xi_{it}\right)+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\bm{J}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\xi_{it}\right)+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\bm{J}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\xi_{it}\right)+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle\to_{d}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0}_{r},(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{t}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})^{-1}\right).

(71)

Notice that,
\bm{J}
plays no role in the covariance since
\bm{J}
is diagonal and
\bm{J}^{2}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
. From (
9
) it is clear that the PC estimator is asymptotically equivalent to the OLS estimator,
{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}
, we get when, for fixed
t
, we regress
x_{it}
onto
\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}
. Indeed,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{J}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}x_{it}\right)-\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{J}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{J}\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}+\xi_{it})\right)-\bm{J}\mathbf{F}_{t}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{J}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{J}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\xi_{it}\right)

\displaystyle=\bm{J}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\xi_{it}\right).

(72)

Appendix A
Proofs of of main results

A.1
Proof of Proposition
1

First notice that
\text{rk}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)=r
for all
n
, since
\text{rk}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})=r
by Assumption
1
(b) and
\text{rk}\left(\frac{\bm{\Gamma}^{\chi}}{n}\right)=r
by Lemma
1
(iv).
Indeed,
\text{rk}\left(\frac{\bm{\Gamma}^{\chi}}{n}\right)\leq\min(\text{rk}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F}),\text{rk}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right))
.
This holds for all
n>N
and since eigenvalues are an increasing sequence in
n
. Therefore,
(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n})^{-1}
is well defined for all
n
and
\bm{\Lambda}
admits a left inverse.

Second, since

\frac{\bm{\Gamma}^{\chi}}{n}=\mathbf{V}^{\chi}\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\mathbf{V}^{\chi\prime}=\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{n}}.

(A1)

the columns of
\frac{\mathbf{V}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{n}}
and the columns of
\frac{\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{n}}
must span the same space.

So there exists an
r\times r
invertible matrix
\mathbf{K}
such that

{\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}}\mathbf{K}={\mathbf{V}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}}

(A2)

Therefore, from (
A2
)

\mathbf{K}=(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}

(A3)

which is also obtained by linear projection, and also

\mathbf{K}^{-1}=(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{-1/2}{\mathbf{V}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}

(A4)

which are both finite and positive definite because of Lemma
9
.

Now, from (
A2
) and (
A3
)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{V}}^{\chi}({\mathbf{M}}^{\chi})^{1/2}=\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\mathbf{K}=\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}

(A5)

Let

\bm{\mathcal{H}}=(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}\mathbf{J},

(A6)

which is finite and positive definite because of Lemma
10
.

Now,
because of Lemmas
5
(iii),
5
(iv),
7
(i), using (
13
), (
A5
), and (
A6
),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|=

\displaystyle\,\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{x}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{1/2}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\chi}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{M}}^{\chi}}{n}\right)^{1/2}\mathbf{J}\right\|=\ \left\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{x}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{1/2}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\chi}\mathbf{J}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{M}}^{\chi}}{n}\right)^{1/2}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{x}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\chi}\mathbf{J}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right\|+\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left\{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}\right)^{1/2}-\left(\mathbf{M}^{\chi}\right)^{1/2}\right\}\right\|\,\left\|\mathbf{V}^{\chi}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{V}}^{x}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\chi}\mathbf{J}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left\{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}\right)^{1/2}-\left(\mathbf{M}^{\chi}\right)^{1/2}\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}},\frac{1}{T}\right)\right),

since
\|\mathbf{V}^{\chi}\|=1
because eigenvectors are normalized.
This proves part (a).

For part (b), since from (
13
), for any
i=1,\ldots,n
, we have

\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}=\widehat{\mathbf{v}}^{x\prime}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x})^{1/2},

(A7)

then, because of Lemma
5
(iii),
6
(ii), and
6
(iii), using (
A5
) and (
A7
),

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\mathcal{H}}\right\|=

\displaystyle\,\left\|\sqrt{n}\widehat{\mathbf{v}}^{x\prime}_{i}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{1/2}-\sqrt{n}{\mathbf{v}}_{i}^{\chi\prime}\mathbf{J}\left(\frac{{\mathbf{M}}^{\chi}}{n}\right)^{1/2}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\sqrt{n}\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{i}^{x\prime}-\sqrt{n}{\mathbf{v}}_{i}^{\chi\prime}\mathbf{J}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right\|+\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left\{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}\right)^{1/2}-\left(\mathbf{M}^{\chi}\right)^{1/2}\right\}\right\|\,\left\|\sqrt{n}\mathbf{v}_{i}^{\chi\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|\sqrt{n}\widehat{\mathbf{v}}_{i}^{x\prime}-\sqrt{n}{\mathbf{v}}_{i}^{\chi\prime}\mathbf{J}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left\{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}\right)^{1/2}-\left(\mathbf{M}^{\chi}\right)^{1/2}\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{T}\right)\right).

This proves part (b).

For part (c), using (
14
)

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{F}}

\displaystyle=\bm{X}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}})^{-1}=\frac{\bm{X}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}=\frac{\bm{X}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}+\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})}{n}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{n}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}+\left\{\frac{\bm{X}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})}{n}+\frac{\bm{\Xi}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{n}\right\}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}.

(A8)

Now, from (
A5
) and (
A6
)

\bm{\mathcal{H}}=(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\mathbf{K}\mathbf{J},

(A9)

and so from (
A9
)

\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{-1}=\mathbf{J}\mathbf{K}^{-1}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}.

(A10)

which is finite and positive definite because of Lemma
10
(ii). From (
A10
) and (
A4
):

\displaystyle\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{n}\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}\mathbf{J}\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}^{\chi}\left(\mathbf{M}^{\chi}\right)^{-1/2}\mathbf{J}=(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}(\mathbf{K}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{J}=(\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{-1})^{\prime}.

(A11)

Then, because of Lemma
5
(iii),
2
(i),
7
(ii),
7
(iv), and
10
(i), by using (
A11
),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{n}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}-(\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{-1})^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{n}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}-\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{n}\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right\|\,\left\|\bm{\mathcal{H}}\right\|\,\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}-\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right\|\,\left\|\bm{\mathcal{H}}\right\|\,\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}-\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right\|\,\left\|\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right).

(A12)

By using (
A12
) in (
A8
), because of part (a), Lemma
2
(ii),
2
(iv),
3
(ii), and Lemma
7
(iv), and
10
(i)

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{F}}-\bm{F}(\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{-1})^{\prime}}{\sqrt{T}}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{n}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}-(\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{-1})^{\prime}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{F}}{\sqrt{T}}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\{\left\|\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{X}}{\sqrt{nT}}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\bm{\Xi}\bm{\Lambda}}{n\sqrt{T}}\right\|\,\|\bm{\mathcal{H}}\|^{2}\right\}\cdot\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

This proves part (c).

Similarly, for part (d), from (
14
) and (
A8
), for any
t=1,\ldots,T
, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}

\displaystyle=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{t}=\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{t}}{n}+\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\left\{\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{t}}{n}+\frac{\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t}}{n}\right\}.

(A13)

Because of Assumption
1
(a) and Lemma
1
(ii), we have

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t}}{n}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\xi_{it}\lambda_{ik}\right)^{2}\right]=\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\right]\lambda_{ik}\lambda_{jk}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{rM_{\Lambda}^{2}}{n^{2}}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}\left[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\right]|\leq\frac{rM_{\Lambda}^{2}}{n^{2}}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{j=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}\left[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\right]|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{rM_{\Lambda}^{2}M_{\xi}}{n},

(A14)

since
M_{\xi}
is independent of
t
. This is a special case of Lemma
3
(ii).

Therefore, by using (
A12
) and (
A14
) in (
A13
), because of part (a), Lemma
2
(ii),
2
(iv),
7
(iv),
10
(i)

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\frac{\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}-\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{-1}\right\|\,\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\{\left\|\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\mathbf{x}_{t}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\ \bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t}}{n}\right\|\,\|\bm{\mathcal{H}}\|^{2}\right\}\cdot\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

This proves part (d) and it completes the proof.
\Box

A.2
Proof of Proposition
2

For part (a), for any
i=1,\ldots,n
, by Assumption
1
(a),

\left\|\frac{1}{nT}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\right\|\leq\left\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\right\|\leq M_{\Lambda}\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\right\|.

(A15)

Then, by Assumptions
1
(a) and
4

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right]=\frac{1}{n^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{h=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{kt}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{jt}[\bm{\Lambda}_{jh}]\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r^{2}}{n^{2}T^{2}}\max_{h,k=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[F_{kt}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{jt}[\bm{\Lambda}_{jh}]\right)F_{ks}\left(\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}\xi_{\ell s}[\bm{\Lambda}_{\ell h}]\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{2}}{n^{2}T^{2}}\max_{h,k=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[F_{kt}F_{ks}]\,\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi_{jt}\xi_{\ell s}\right][\bm{\Lambda}_{jh}][\bm{\Lambda}_{\ell h}]

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\frac{r^{2}}{T}\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[F_{kt}F_{ks}\right]\right\}\,\left\{\max_{t,s=1\ldots,T}\frac{M_{\Lambda}^{2}}{n^{2}T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}\left|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{jt}\xi_{\ell s}]\right|\right\}.

(A16)

Now, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, for any
k=1,\ldots,r
,

\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}F_{kt}F_{ks}\right|\leq\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{kt}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}F_{ks}^{2}\right)^{1/2},

(A17)

and, by Assumption
1
(b), using (
A17
) and again Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[F_{kt}F_{ks}]

\displaystyle=\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}F_{kt}F_{ks}\right]\leq\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}F_{kt}F_{ks}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{kt}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}F_{ks}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{kt}^{2}\right)\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}F_{ks}^{2}\right)\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[F_{kt}^{2}]\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[F_{ks}^{2}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[F_{kt}^{2}]\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\mathbb{E}[F_{kt}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\bm{\eta}_{k}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}_{F}\bm{\eta}_{k}\leq\|\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\|\leq M_{F},

(A18)

since
M_{F}
is independent of
t
and where
\bm{\eta}_{k}
is an
r
-dimensional vector with one in the
k
th entry and zero elsewhere. And, because of Lemma
1
(ii)

\max_{t,s=1,\ldots,T}\frac{1}{n^{2}T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}\left|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{jt}\xi_{\ell s}]\right|\leq\frac{M_{\xi}}{nT},

(A19)

since
M_{\xi}
is independent of
n
and
t
.
By substituting (
A18
) and (
A19
) into (
A16
),

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{r^{2}M_{F}M_{\Lambda}^{2}M_{\xi}}{nT}.

(A20)

By substituting (
A20
) into (
A15
), we prove part (a).

For part (b), for any
i=1,\ldots,n
, because of Lemma
2
(i),

\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{j}\bm{\lambda}_{j}^{\prime}\right\|=\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})\right\|\leq\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|^{2}\leq\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|M_{\Lambda}^{2}.

(A21)

Then, by Assumptions
4
and
2
(b) and using (
A18
)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}F_{jt}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r}{T^{2}}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}F_{jt}\xi_{is}F_{js}]=\frac{r}{T^{2}}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}F_{js}]\,\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}]

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\frac{r}{T}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}F_{js}]\right\}\left\{\frac{1}{T}\max_{t,s=1,\ldots,n}\left|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}]\right|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq rM_{F}\frac{M_{ii}}{T}\max_{t,s=1,\ldots,n}\rho^{|t-s|}\leq\frac{rM_{F}M_{\xi}}{T},

(A22)

since
M_{\xi}
is independent of
i
. Or, equivalently, by Lemma
1
(iii) and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r}{T^{2}}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}F_{js}]\,\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}]

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\frac{r}{T}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}\max_{t,s=1,\ldots,n}|\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}F_{js}]|\right\}\left\{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\left|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}]\right|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r}{T}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}\max_{t,s=1,\ldots,n}\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]\frac{M_{\xi}(1+\rho)}{1-\rho}\leq\frac{rM_{F}M_{3\xi}}{T},

(A23)

since
M_{F}
is independent of
t
and
M_{3\xi}
is independent of
i
. Notice that
M_{\xi}\leq M_{3\xi}
. Notice that (
A23
) is a special case of Lemma
3
(i). By substituting (
A22
), or (
A23
), into (
A21
), we prove part (b).

For part (c), for any
i=1,\ldots,n
, because of Assumption
1
(a),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{j}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{r}\left(\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\lambda_{jk}\right)^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\leq\sqrt{r}M_{\Lambda}\left|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}M_{\Lambda}\left\{\left|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\{\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right\}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right|\right\}.

(A24)

Then, by Assumption
2
(b),

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right|\leq\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right|\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right|\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}M_{ij}\leq\frac{M_{\xi}}{n},

(A25)

since
M_{\xi}
is independent of
i
and
t
. Moreover, by Assumption
2
(c),

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\{\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right\}\right|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{K_{\xi}}{nT}.

(A26)

By substituting (
A25
) and (
A26
) into (
A24
), we prove part (c).

For part (d), for any
i=1,\ldots,n
, because of Assumption
1
(a)

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{nT}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\bm{\mathcal{H}})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq M_{\Lambda}\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\bm{\mathcal{H}})\right\|

\displaystyle=M_{\Lambda}\left\|\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})}{nT}\right\|\leq M_{\Lambda}\left\|\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|.

(A27)

Then, by using Lemma
3
(i) and Proposition
1
(a) in (
A27
), we prove part (d).

For part (e), for any
i=1,\ldots,n
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{j}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\bm{\mathcal{H}})\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|.

(A28)

By substituting part (ii), Lemma
2
(i), and part (a) of Proposition
1
into (
A28
), we prove part (e).

Finally, for part (f), for any
i=1,\ldots,n
, let
\bm{\zeta}_{i}=(\xi_{i1}\cdots\xi_{iT})^{\prime}
, then

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{nT}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{\prime}\bm{\mathcal{H}})\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{\bm{\zeta}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}\right)}{nT}\right\|\leq\left\|\frac{\bm{\zeta}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|.

(A29)

Then, by the
C_{r}
-inequality with
r=2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\bm{\zeta}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\bm{\xi}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right\}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left\{\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right\}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right)^{2}\right\}

(A30)

By taking the expectation of (
A30
), and because of Assumption
2
(c) and Lemma
1
(v),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\frac{\bm{\zeta}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right\}\right)^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\max_{j=1,\ldots,n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right\}\right)^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2K_{\xi}}{T}+\frac{2}{nT^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{is}\xi_{js}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2K_{\xi}}{T}+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\right|\max_{s=1,\ldots,T}\max_{i,j=1,\ldots,n}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{is}\xi_{js}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2K_{\xi}}{T}+\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}M_{ij}\max_{i,j=1,\ldots,n}\bm{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}^{\xi}\bm{\varepsilon}_{j}\leq\frac{2K_{\xi}}{T}+\frac{2M_{\xi}}{n}\|\bm{\Gamma}^{\xi}\|\leq\frac{2K_{\xi}}{T}+\frac{2M_{\xi}^{2}}{n},

(A31)

since
K_{\xi}
is independent of
j
and
M_{\xi}
is independent of
i
,
j
,
t
, and
s
and where
\bm{\varepsilon}_{i}
is an
n
-dimensional vector with one in the
i
th entry and zero elsewhere.
By substituting (
A31
) and part (a) of Proposition
1
into (
A29
), we prove part (f). This completes the proof.
\Box

A.3
Proof of Proposition
3

Part (a), is proved using Proposition

2
in (
30
) and since
\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}(1)
because of Lemma
7
(iv) and
10
(i).

For part (b), from (
29
)

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left(\left\|\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\frac{\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}\bm{\Lambda}}{n^{3/2}T}\right\|\right)\left\|\bm{\mathcal{H}}\right\|\,\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left(\left\|\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{nT}\right\|\right)\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\right\|,

and the proof of part (b) follows from
Proposition
1
(a) and Lemma

2
(i),

3
(i),

3
(iii),

3
(iv),

7
(iv),

10
(i).

For part (c), because of part (b), and Lemma
2
(i) and
11
(i),
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{n}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}+\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime})(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}+\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}})}{n}-\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{n}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\,\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{H}}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{nT}}\right)\right).

This proves part (c) and completes the proof.
\Box

A.4
Proof of Proposition
4

Start with part (a). From (
A6
) and (
A4
)

\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)=\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}\mathbf{V}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)=\mathbf{J}\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)^{1/2}\frac{\mathbf{V}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}=\mathbf{J}\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}.

(A32)

Then, from (
A3
)

\mathbf{V}^{\chi}=\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\mathbf{K}({\mathbf{M}^{\chi}})^{-1/2}

(A33)

thus, from (
A33
) and (
A4
)

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{-1/2}{\mathbf{V}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)({\mathbf{M}^{\chi}})^{-1}\mathbf{K}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}\mathbf{K}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}.

(A34)

And, from (
A3
)

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}\mathbf{K}\mathbf{K}^{\prime}\mathbf{J}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}(\mathbf{M}^{\chi})^{1/2}\mathbf{V}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\mathbf{J}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}^{\chi}\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\mathbf{J}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda})^{-1}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\mathbf{J}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\mathbf{J}=\mathbf{I}_{r}.

(A35)

Therefore, the columns of
\mathbf{J}\mathbf{K}
are the normalized eigenvectors of
(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}
with eigenvalues
\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}
(notice that
\mathbf{J}\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)=\left(\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\right)\mathbf{J}
). Moreover, by Assumption
1
(a)

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\|(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}-(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\right\|=0.

(A36)

Letting,
\bm{V}_{0}
be the matrix of eigenvalues of
(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}
sorted in descending order, from (
A36
) we also have (this is proved also in Lemma
8
(i))

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\|\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}-\bm{V}_{0}\right\|=0.

(A37)

Let
\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}
be the normalized eigenvectors of
(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}

Hence, by continuity of eigenvectors, and since the eigenvalues
\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}
are distinct because of Assumption
3
, from Theorem 2 in
Yu
et al. 2015
by Lemma
8
(iii) and using (
A36
) it follows that

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\|\mathbf{J}\mathbf{K}-\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}\bm{\mathcal{J}}_{0}\right\|\leq\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{2^{3/2}\sqrt{r}\left\|(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}-(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\right\|}{\mu_{r}(\bm{V}_{0})}=0,

(A38)

where
\bm{\mathcal{J}}_{0}
is an
r\times r
diagonal matrix with entries
\pm 1
, which is in general different from
\mathbf{J}
. Finally, since
\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}
is an orthogonal matrix, we have
\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{-1}=\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{\prime}
, so from (
A38
)

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\|\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{J}-\bm{\mathcal{J}}_{0}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{\prime}\right\|=0.

(A39)

By using (
A39
) and (
A37
) into (
A32
) and since
\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{J}=\mathbf{J}\mathbf{K}^{-1}
, we have

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)-\bm{V}_{0}\bm{\mathcal{J}}_{0}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}\right\|=0.

By defining
\bm{Q}_{0}=\bm{V}_{0}\bm{\mathcal{J}}_{0}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1/2}
, we prove part (a).

Part (b) follows from Proposition
1
(a) and Lemma
2
(i), and since

\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}-\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right\|\leq\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}-\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|=o_{\mathrm{P}}(1).

(A40)

Part (c) follows from parts (a) and (b) and Proposition
1
(a) and since

\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}-\bm{Q}_{0}\right\|\leq\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}-\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right\|+\left\|\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\frac{{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}-\bm{Q}_{0}\right\|=o_{\mathrm{P}}(1).

(A41)

This completes the proof.
\Box

A.5
Alternative proof of Proposition
4
(c)

Recall (
26
), i.e.,

\frac{\bm{X}^{\prime}\bm{X}}{nT}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}=\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}.

(A42)

Then, from (

) we have

\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\frac{\bm{X}^{\prime}\bm{X}}{nT}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}.

(A43)

Then, substituting
\bm{X}^{\prime}\bm{X}=(\bm{\Lambda}\bm{F}^{\prime}+\bm{\Xi}^{\prime})^{\prime}(\bm{F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}+\bm{\Xi})
into (
A43
)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}+\bm{D}_{nT}=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n},

(A44)

where

\displaystyle\bm{D}_{nT}=

\displaystyle\,\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left\{\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{nT}+\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}}{n}\frac{\bm{F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{nT}+\frac{1}{nT}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left\{\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{nT}+\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}}{n}\frac{\bm{F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{nT}+\frac{1}{nT}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right\}\bm{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left\{\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})}{nT}+\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}}{n}\frac{\bm{F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})}{nT}+\frac{1}{nT}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})}{n}\right\}.

(A45)

Let,

\bm{B}_{nT}=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2},\hskip 18.49988pt\bm{R}_{nT}=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right)

(A46)

Then, notice that because of Proposition
1
(a) and Lemma
2
(i) and
3
(v)

\left\|\bm{R}_{nT}-\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{n}\right)\right\|\leq\left\|\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right).

Moreover,
\|\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}-\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\|=o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)
because of Lemma

(i), with
\bm{\Gamma}^{F}
positive definite because of Assumption

because of Assumption

positive definite because of Assumption

(a) and Lemma

\|\bm{R}_{nT}^{-1}\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}(1)
. Therefore, (

) can be written as

are the non-normalized eigenvectors of

. which has as normalized eigenvectors the columns of

so that we can write

(A48)

.
Thus, (

) can be written as:

\displaystyle\left(\bm{B}_{nT}+\bm{D}_{nT}\bm{R}_{nT}^{-1}\right)\bm{\Upsilon}_{nT}=\bm{\Upsilon}_{nT}\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right).

Now, from (

,

,

\displaystyle\|\bm{D}_{nT}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\right\|\,\left\{\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}}{n\sqrt{T}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{F}}{\sqrt{T}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{nT}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|^{2}\right\}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{H}}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\right\|\left\{\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{\sqrt{n}T}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}}{n\sqrt{T}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{F}}{\sqrt{T}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{nT}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|\right\}\,\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{nT}}\right)\right)=o_{\mathrm{P}}(1).

(A49)

While because of Lemma
4
(i) and Assumption

(a),

\left\|\bm{B}_{nT}-(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\right\|=o_{\mathrm{P}}(1).

(A50)

Hence, by continuity of eigenvectors, and since the eigenvalues

by Lemma

\left\|\bm{\Upsilon}_{nT}-\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}\bm{\mathcal{J}}_{0}\right\|\leq\frac{2^{3/2}\sqrt{r}\left\|\bm{B}_{nT}-(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\right\|}{\mu_{r}(\bm{V}_{0})}=o_{\mathrm{P}}(1),

(A51)

where
\bm{\mathcal{J}}_{0}
is an

diagonal matrix with entries
\pm 1
.

Furthermore, from (

) and (

)

\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right)=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{-1/2}\bm{R}_{nT}=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{-1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{nT}(\bm{V}_{nT})^{1/2}.

(A52)

Notice that
\left\|\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{-1/2}\right\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}(1)
because of Lemma

(b).

Now, by Proposition

(a), Lemma

(i) and

A6
)

\displaystyle\bm{R}_{nT}\bm{R}_{nT}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right)\left(\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}\right)\bm{\mathcal{H}}\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\left(\frac{{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}^{\chi\prime}\frac{\mathbf{M}^{\chi}}{n}\mathbf{V}^{\chi}\bm{\Lambda}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\frac{\bm{\Gamma}^{\chi}}{n}\bm{\Lambda}\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\left(\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right)\left(\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{T}\right)^{1/2}+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1)

\displaystyle=(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}+o_{\mathrm{P}}(1).

because of Lemma

(a). Since the eigenvalues of

, then, from (

(A54)

Finally, from (

), because of Lemma

) and (

\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}-(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{1/2}\bm{\Upsilon}_{0}\bm{V}_{0}\bm{\mathcal{J}}_{0}\right\|=\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}}{n}-\bm{Q}_{0}^{\prime}\right\|=o_{\mathrm{P}}(1).

A.6
Proof of Proposition
5

For part (a):

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1})\mathbf{F}_{t}}{n}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}})\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}}{n}

\displaystyle=-\left\{\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}})^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{n}+\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}})}{n}\right\}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t},

(A55)

Then, from (
A55
)

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1})\mathbf{F}_{t}}{n}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\left\|\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}})^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{n}\right\|+\left\|\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}})}{n}\right\|\right\}\,\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=\left\{\bm{I}+\bm{II}\right\}\,\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|,\;\text{say.}

(A56)

First, consider
\bm{I}
in (
A56
). From (
29
)

\displaystyle\bm{I}=

\displaystyle\,\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}})^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\frac{\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{nT}+\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{nT}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{n}+\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{H}}^{\prime}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{n^{2}T}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})^{\prime}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}}{nT}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}+\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})^{\prime}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}\bm{F}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{nT}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\frac{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}-\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\mathcal{H}})^{\prime}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}}{nT}\frac{\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{\sqrt{n}}=\bm{I}_{a}+\bm{I}_{b}+\bm{I}_{c}+\bm{I}_{d}+\bm{I}_{e}+\bm{I}_{f},\;\text{say.}

(A57)

(A59)

\displaystyle\|\bm{I}_{b}\|\leq\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\mathbf{M}}^{x}}{n}\right)^{-1}\right\|\,\left\|\bm{\mathcal{H}}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Xi}^{\prime}\bm{F}}{nT}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}}{n}\right\|\,\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{H}}\right\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nT}}\right).

-dimensional vector with one in the
